  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软电容作为驱动器和传感器具有重要应用价值，但其制造过程中的介电层形成与电极应用等关键工艺主要依赖人工操作，存在效率低、一致性差、人工参与度过高等问题，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出自动化机器人制造系统，核心优化两个关键工艺：利用旋涂法进行介电层形成，利用冲压法进行碳纳米管电极应用；通过机器人自动化集成完成多层DEA的组装制造。

Result: 成功制造了20个多层DEA，每个含12个介电层（平均厚度55.37±2.04 μm）和11个交替CNT电极；总工艺时间减少14.2%，操作员参与时间减少56.1%，证明了自动化系统的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 自动化机器人制造系统能够显著提升软电容（DEA）的制造效率并降低人工干预，为软驱动器和传感器的规模化、一致性生产提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This letter presents an automated robotic manufacturing system for soft capacitors which operate as actuators and sensors. Emphasis is placed on the two processes that most directly govern device quality, dielectric layer formation by spin coating and carbon nanotube (CNT) electrode application by stamping. Twenty multilayer DEAs, each comprising 12 dielectric layers with a mean thickness of 55.37 +- 2.04 um and 11 alternating CNT electrodes, were fabricated reducing total process time by 14.2% and removing the operator from 56.1% of it.

</details>


### [64] [The Gate, Not the Cache: Gate Provenance Bounds the Closed-Loop Reliability of Training-Free VLA Token Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00391)
*Qi Luo,Shuaijun Liu,Hao Zhao,Kunlin Li,Xiaobo Wang,Ningxing Su,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中token跳过机制的闭环可靠性问题，发现门控信号来源（是否来自干净的密集前向传播）而非跳过机制本身决定了加速VLA的表现。作者提出actuation-slack refresh，在关键路径外执行一次密集前向传播来修复门控和KV缓存，使缓存和剪枝方法在LIBERO和SIMPLER任务上从崩溃恢复至0.98的成功率，并降低18-22%的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: token skipping是一种广泛使用的免训练加速VLA模型的方法，通过在每一步控制中门控跳过大多数视觉token的计算。然而当门控信号从先前加速前向传播中获取时，被跳过的token也是下一个门控最不可见的token，这种损伤会在控制步骤间累积（compounding），最终导致任务失败。虽然已有工作研究token跳过，但对门控信号来源这个关键因素的系统分析缺失，且现有加速方法上的性能崩溃对动作级检测器不可见，难以被事先发现。

Method: 1) 系统交叉研究reuse和deletion两种机制与门控信号来源（密集前向 vs 自加速前向）的组合；2) 提出actuation-slack refresh：在机器人执行当前动作chunk时，利用执行空隙在关键路径之外执行一次密集前向传播，为下一步提供干净的门控（clean gate）和全新的KV基础（fresh KV base）；3) 由于检测器不可靠地揭示失败，刷新采用无条件执行而非触发式；4) 将刷新集成到state-of-the-art缓存和剪枝方法中，在两个VLA策略、4个LIBERO套件和4个SIMPLER任务上验证。

Result: 在LIBERO-Object上设置跳过率0.9时，当门控来自模型自身加速前向时，reuse机制成功率降至0.68、deletion机制降至0.31（对比密集基线1.00），且该崩溃对动作级检测器不可见。采用actuation-slack refresh后，两种机制均恢复至0.98，同时保持跳过的速度和密集前向的信息。端到端延迟比密集基线降低18-22%，并在仿真和物理机器人上均得到验证。刷新修复了所有因使用自回收门控导致的崩溃。

Conclusion: 对于加速VLA模型，决定闭环可靠性的关键因素不是token如何被跳过（机制层面），而是门控信号从哪里来（是否来自未跳过任何token的干净前向传播）。actuation-slack refresh通过利用动作执行的固有间隙在关键路径外进行密集前向传播，同时获得速度收益和密集信息保真，是解决自回收门控导致的累积损伤的有效方案。由于现有动作级检测器无法揭示此类故障，无条件刷新优于条件触发式刷新。

Abstract: Token skipping is a widely used training-free way to accelerate vision--language--action (VLA) models by bypassing computation for most visual tokens at each control step according to a gate. When the next gate is harvested from the previous accelerated forward, however, the tokens skipped at one step are also the ones least visible to the next gate, and the damage can compound across control steps until the task fails. We study the two mechanisms this class is built on, reuse and deletion, crossing each against where its gate signal comes from on identical episodes. At a skip ratio of 0.9 on LIBERO-Object, both collapse when the gate comes from the model's own accelerated forwards, to 0.68 under reuse and to 0.31 under deletion against a dense 1.00, and the collapse is invisible to the action-level detectors we evaluate. What separates collapse from dense-level operation is not the mechanism but whether the gate is clean, computed by a forward that skipped nothing. We therefore propose actuation-slack refresh, one dense pass run while the robot executes its current action chunk, off the critical path, that hands the next step a clean gate and a fresh KV base. Since the measured detectors do not reliably reveal the failure, the refresh is unconditional rather than triggered. Both mechanisms then recover to 0.98, keeping the speed of skipping and the information of a dense pass. We then integrate the refresh into state-of-the-art caching and pruning methods across two VLA policies, 4 LIBERO suites, and 4 SIMPLER tasks, where it repairs every collapse caused by using a self-harvested gate. Serve latency drops 18--22\% below dense, measured both in simulation and on a physical robot. Where the gate signal comes from, not how tokens are skipped, decides closed-loop reliability for accelerated VLAs.

</details>


### [65] [Embodied Passive Aeroacoustic Perception Enables Relative Sensing and Pursuit Between Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00401)
*Yanbaihui Liu,Ravi Prakash,Li-Yu Lo,Nils Roede,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的感知范式——具身被动气动声学感知（embodied passive aeroacoustic perception），并开发了SonicFly框架，使无人机仅通过聆听另一架无人机的自然飞行声波，在无主动声学信号、无通信、无GPS的情况下实现相对定位与跟随。实验表明该框架在强自身声学干扰和复杂户外环境下，实现了平均1.34米的距离保持误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人在飞行时会产生结构化的气动声学场，但这类信号此前未被充分探索作为机载相对感知的信息源。特别是在户外多机同时飞行时，强烈的自身声学干扰使得利用他人飞行声音进行感知极具挑战，因此作者希望探索这种自然产生的声学信号作为相对感知信息来源的可行性。

Method: SonicFly框架包含以下核心组件：1）轻量级四麦克风阵列用于采集声学信号；2）基于旋翼飞行器特征的气动声学表示（rotorcraft-informed acoustic representations）；3）神经方位-距离估计器（neural bearing-range estimator）用于从声学特征中提取相对状态；4）置信度门控滤波（confidence-gated filtering）用于闭环飞行控制。系统在无主动声学信号、无通信、无GPS共享、无外部感知基础设施的条件下实现跟随。

Result: 通过声学表征、机载定位和户外追踪实验，证明了多旋翼气动声学信号在强自身声学干扰、环境多变和飞行几何变化下仍包含足够的相对感知信息。在纯声学跟随模式下，SonicFly在多样户外轨迹和操作条件下达到了1.34米的平均距离保持误差。此外，对声学通道的分析揭示了谐波结构、频谱可分离性和空间声学线索在决定可观测性中的设计原则。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了具身被动气动声学感知对空中机器人的可行性，表明自然产生的行为学信号（飞行声音）可以作为机器人感知和协调的信息来源，为无人机编队和协同感知提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Aerial robots generate structured aeroacoustic fields during flight, yet these signals have been underexplored as a source of onboard relative perception, particularly under the strong ego-acoustic interference generated during simultaneous flight in various outdoor conditions. We introduce embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, a sensing paradigm in which an aerial robot infers actionable relative-state information from the naturally generated sound of flight while operating within its own evolving aeroacoustic field. We present SonicFly, a passive aeroacoustic perception framework that enables one unmanned aerial vehicle to estimate and follow another using only the leader's intrinsic flight sound, without active acoustic signaling, inter-robot communication, GPS sharing, or external sensing infrastructure. The system uses a lightweight four-microphone array, rotorcraft-informed acoustic representations, a neural bearing-range estimator, and confidence-gated filtering for closed-loop flight. Through acoustic characterization, onboard localization, and outdoor pursuit experiments, we show that multirotor aeroacoustic signals contain sufficient information to support relative perception despite strong ego-acoustic interference, environmental variability, and changing flight geometry. During acoustic-only pursuit, SonicFly achieved a mean distance-maintenance error of 1.34 m across diverse outdoor trajectories and operating conditions. Analysis of the acoustic channel reveals design principles governing embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, including the roles of harmonic structure, spectral separability, and spatial acoustic cues in determining observability. Our results establish the feasibility of embodied passive aeroacoustic perception for aerial robots and suggest that naturally generated behavioral signals can serve as information for robotic perception and coordination.

</details>


### [66] [Learning to Predict Contact Force Distributions from Vision Leveraging Object Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00464)
*Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domaea,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Abdullah Mustafa,Floris Erich,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的方法，通过统计平滑机器人仿真器输出的点力，并结合物体几何形状引导平滑过程，使机器人能够像人类一样基于视觉做出粗粒度的物理预测。该方法在仿真和真实环境中均表现出色，仅用仿真数据训练即可泛化到真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以基于视觉和先验经验对物理交互做出大致预测并调整操作策略，但机器人目前缺乏类似能力。此外，仿真器输出的点力是带噪声的，而人类即使在不熟悉的环境下也能做出连贯一致的预测。作者因此假设：预测平滑的力分布而非原始点力，可以同时提升力预测本身的质量和下游任务表现。

Method: 1) 使用机器人领域常用的刚体仿真器收集视觉与力的配对数据；2) 对仿真器输出的点力施加统计平滑生成目标力分布；3) 在平滑过程中融入物体几何信息以适应接触状态的差异，使预测更加一致；4) 构建一个从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的广泛评估表明：所提方法提升了预测准确性，通过平滑增强了下游任务表现，几何引导的平滑进一步带来了额外收益。值得注意的是，尽管仅使用仿真数据训练，模型能有效泛化到真实世界场景。

Conclusion: 预测平滑的三维力分布比直接预测原始点力更有效，几何引导的平滑能进一步改善预测的连贯性和下游任务性能，且该模型具备从仿真到真实的良好迁移能力。

Abstract: Based on vision and prior experience, humans can make rough physical predictions and adjust their manipulation strategies. This paper aims to endow robots with a similar ability. To collect paired data of vision and forces, we use a rigid-body simulator commonly adopted in robotics. However, unlike simulators that output noisy point forces, humans are able to make consistent predictions even in unfamiliar situations. Based on this observation, we hypothesize that predicting smooth force distributions rather than raw point forces can improve both force prediction itself and downstream task performance. To validate this hypothesis, we construct a model that predicts three-dimensional force distributions from a single RGB image of piled daily objects. The target distribution is generated by applying statistical smoothing to point forces obtained from the simulator. Moreover, by incorporating object geometry into the smoothing process, we aim to account for variations in contact states and achieve more consistent vision-based predictions. We conduct extensive evaluations in both simulation and real environments. Results show that our approach improves prediction accuracy, enhances downstream task performance through smoothing, and further benefits from geometry-guided smoothing. Remarkably, the trained model generalizes effectively to real-world scenes despite being trained solely in simulation.

</details>


### [67] [From Digital to Physical Reservoir Computing: Co-Optimizing Soft Robotic Reservoirs via Dynamics Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00484)
*Nicola Visentin,Maximilian Stölzle,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Francesco Braghin,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对软机器人物理储层计算系统进行动力学级预训练/协同优化的框架，通过联合优化物理参数、微分同胚状态映射和前馈-反馈控制，使物理储层的动力学逼近高性能数字参考动力学。在四个任务（sMNIST、ADIAC分类；Mackey-Glass、Lorenz96预测）上，优化后的软机器人储层相比未优化的基线平均相对提升33.7%，并接近数字参考性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软机器人物理储层计算（PRC）通常将物理系统"按原样"使用，不做预训练或协同优化，导致其性能可能低于数字储层。现有方法缺乏将物理储层与高性能数字参考动力学对齐的系统性训练框架，因此需要探索如何对物理储层进行预训练以提升其计算能力。

Method: 提出一个联合优化框架，包含三个关键组成部分：(1) 物理参数优化；(2) 微分同胚的物理-参考状态映射函数学习；(3) 前馈-反馈控制信号的优化。优化采用可微物理模型和加速度级方程误差目标（避免时间积分带来的数值问题），并以随机振荡器网络（RON）作为数字参考动力学。算法上采用并行多起点梯度下降方法，在模拟软机器人上实现。

Result: 在四个储层维度、四类任务（sMNIST/ADIAC分类、Mackey-Glass/Lorenz96预测）上，优化后的软机器人储层相比未优化储层平均相对提升33.7%，性能接近数字参考（RON）储层。实验验证了动力学级协同优化在模拟软机器人储层上的可行性。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了物理储层（软机器人）可以通过动力学级预训练/协同优化显著提升性能，缩小与数字储层的差距。这为软机器人物理计算系统的设计提供了新的方法论，暗示"设计物理系统本身"而非仅使用现成系统，是提升PRC性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Soft robotic substrates are promising for Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) because their compliant nonlinear dynamics can provide temporal memory, high-dimensional state transformations, and efficient inference. However, physical reservoirs are often adopted as-is rather than pretrained or co-optimized, potentially limiting soft robotic PRC performance relative to digital reservoirs. We investigate whether a physical reservoir can instead be pretrained against high-performing digital reference dynamics. Our formulation jointly optimizes physical parameters, a diffeomorphic physical-reference state map, and feedforward-feedback control using a differentiable physical model and an acceleration-level equation-error objective that avoids temporal integration. As a proof of concept, we instantiate the formulation with simulated soft robots, a Random Oscillators Network (RON) reference, and parallel multi-start gradient descent. We evaluate the optimized reservoirs on classification (sMNIST and ADIAC) and forecasting (Mackey-Glass and Lorenz96) tasks across four reservoir dimensions. Compared with unoptimized soft robot reservoirs, the optimized reservoirs achieve a mean relative improvement of 33.7% across all tasks and datasets, while remaining close to the digital reference. These results demonstrate the feasibility of dynamics-level co-optimization for the simulated soft robotic reservoirs considered here.

</details>


### [68] [First Deployable Dynamic-CoM: A Unified Policy and Method-Agnostic Benchmark for Humanoid Single-Leg Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00500)
*Yikai Zhou,Xingyun Wang,Jieming Cui,Bozhou Chen,Yikai Fan,Yixin Zhu,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FDDC（首个可部署的动态质心观测策略），通过将质心速度（xCoM）构建为可仅由编码器和IMU重构的观测，配合从人体姿势控制逐项翻译的奖励库，使通用人形策略首次实现真正意义上的单腿静态平衡——在90个测试动作中86个保持干净单腿站立，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人上；同时发布首个方法无关、可复现的sim2sim单腿平衡基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的通用人形策略虽然在敏捷全身运动上表现出色，但无法完成简单的单腿站立平衡这一基础能力。八个已发布的SOTA通用策略在90个测试动作中全部无法保持干净的单腿站立，只能通过迈步或跳跃来恢复平衡而非预防失衡。其根本原因是缺乏可部署的捕获点（xCoM）观测——因为质心速度需要机器人基座线速度，而机上传感器无法提供此信息，导致预防性平衡从未在硬件上实现过。

Method: 核心创新包括三方面：(1) 将xCoM（质心速度外推点）相对于支撑脚表达，使基座线速度项精确抵消，从而得到一个可仅由编码器和IMU重构的观测，直接放入在硬件上运行的actor网络中；(2) 构建从人体姿势控制逐项翻译的奖励库，遵循"预防优于修复"原则；(3) 使用不对称FastSAC训练，配特权critic且无需蒸馏。同时还设计了方法无关的sim2sim基准，在不同于训练环境的模拟器中评测各策略。

Result: FDDC在90个留出测试动作中（覆盖九个分层姿态类别）保持了86个干净的单腿平衡，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人上；消融实验表明动态质心观测是最大的单一贡献因素，移除它单独就损失40个干净单腿平衡评分点；相比之下，八个SOTA通用策略在全部90个测试动作中无一能保持干净单腿站立。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，将动态质心信息融入观测空间是解锁人形机器人预防性平衡能力的关键，而此前被忽视的单腿平衡问题可以通过可部署的观测设计和生物启发的奖励机制得到有效解决。发布的sim2sim基准为社区提供了统一、可复现的评测手段，推动平衡从"按任务取巧"转变为"可量化的通用能力"。

Abstract: Unified humanoid policies handle agile whole-body motion, yet stumble on a simple demand: staying balanced on one leg. On our single-leg-balance benchmark, eight released state-of-the-art general policies hold a clean single-leg stance on 0 of 90 test motions; they stay up only by stepping or hopping, recovering from imbalance rather than preventing it. Prevention needs the capture point (xCoM), the center of mass (CoM) extrapolated by its velocity, which has never driven a hardware policy because it requires a base linear velocity no on-board sensor provides; expressed relative to the support foot, that velocity cancels exactly, leaving an observation reconstructible from encoders and IMU alone. We put this first deployable dynamic-CoM observation directly into the actor that runs on hardware, and pair it with a reward library translated term by term from human postural control, under one principle: prevention over repair. Trained by asymmetric FastSAC with a privileged critic and no distillation, the resulting policy, FDDC (First Deployable Dynamic-CoM), holds clean single-leg balance on 86 of 90 held-out motions across nine stratified pose classes and transfers to a real Unitree G1; in ablation, the dynamic-CoM observation is the single largest driver: removing it alone costs 40 points of clean single-leg balance. We release the full stack with the first method-agnostic, reproducible sim2sim benchmark for humanoid single-leg balance, scoring each policy in a simulator distinct from its training one, a step toward turning balance from a per-task trick into a capability the field can measure.

</details>


### [69] [SSTG-Nav: Metric-Grounded Spatial-Semantic Topological Graphs for Reusable Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00527)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SSTG-Nav提出一种可重用的度量-语义记忆系统，通过一次性预探索将ObjectNav从单次探索范式转变为可重复的语义导航，在HM3D-v2基准上实现了0.926的SR和0.603的SPL，并支持Top-3恢复策略达到0.975的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ObjectNav主要被表述为一次性探索问题，无法应对真实部署中机器人在同一环境长期运行的需求。两个核心挑战：1) 识别到物体并不等同于知道可到达的停止位置；2) 单次自信的地图错误即可导致任务终止。缺乏将历史经验转化为可复用导航资产的机制。

Method: SSTG-Nav构建了一个可复用的度量-语义记忆系统，包含三个关键组件：1) 通过一次性调查生成与目标无关的拓扑图，建立目标感知的几何成功上限；2) 跨视角整合证据并固化语义响应；3) 保留空间上可区分的恢复站位（recovery standoffs）。系统还采用了源感知融合（source-aware fusion）和Top-3恢复策略。

Result: 在36个场景的1000个HM3D-v2测试集上：与目标无关的拓扑达到99.4%的几何成功上限；度量锚定将SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.920/0.603；源感知融合达到0.926/0.586；Top-3恢复将Success@1/2/3提升至0.928/0.965/0.975，SPL@3达0.601。消融实验验证了模型、视场角、密度和损坏控制对性能增益的贡献。

Conclusion: 预探索（pre-exploration）是实际可行的实用范式，能够显著提升长期部署下语义导航的可靠性和可重复性。SSTG-Nav通过消除重复空间搜索并引入恢复机制，为服务机器人长期运行提供了可靠的基础设施，且ROS2/Nav2实现证明了从查询到执行的完整管线可行性。

Abstract: Service robots operating for months in the same homes, offices, and facilities should become more reliable with experience instead of searching familiar space from scratch for every request. Yet ObjectNav is predominantly formulated as one-shot exploration, leaving a central deployment challenge unresolved: recognizing an object does not identify a reachable place to stop, and one confident map error can terminate the task. We introduce SSTG-Nav, a reusable metric-semantic memory that turns a one-time survey into actionable object goals, consolidates evidence across viewpoints, and retains spatially distinct recovery standoffs. On 1,000 HM3D-v2 episodes across 36 scenes, our goal-independent topology achieves a 99.4% geometric success ceiling. Holding semantic responses fixed, metric grounding raises SR/SPL from 0.835/0.560 to 0.920/0.603, and source-aware fusion reaches 0.926/0.586. Fusion-aware Top-3 recovery raises Success@1/2/3 to 0.928/0.965/0.975 and reaches 0.601 SPL@3. Model, field-of-view, density, and corruption controls identify where these gains originate, and a ROS2/Nav2 realization demonstrates the complete reusable query-to-execution pipeline. Together, the results establish pre-exploration as a powerful practical regime for dependable, repeated semantic navigation.

</details>


### [70] [Disentangling Visuo-Tactile Foresight: Oracle-Guided Interface Discovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00547)
*Zihang Yao,Chaoyue Ding,Yingying Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF)框架，通过使用来自模拟验证成功轨迹的配对RGB和触觉未来信号来独立研究未来到动作的接口，并提出了不对称相位对齐未来记忆(AFM)方法，在不同的模拟基准上达到32.0%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务依赖于视觉中难以观察的物理交互线索。虽然已有的触觉世界动作模型联合建模未来视觉观测和触觉信号来引导动作生成，但未来信号应如何结构化以供动作专家有效使用这一问题尚未被充分探索。端到端学习难以独立研究该接口，因为其纠缠了物理无效的未来视觉、不可靠的预测、不准确或跨模态不一致的触觉预报，以及不可读的未来到动作接口。

Method: 作者引入了OVTF框架，通过从仿真中验证的成功轨迹提供配对的RGB和触觉未来信号来固定未来提供者，从而隔离接口研究。在该框架内提出了Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM)：视觉记忆读取未来视觉信息；每个触觉记忆联合关注其自身的触觉流和相位对齐的未来视觉；阻断跨触觉访问。并与Modality-Isolated Future Memory (IFM)进行对照，后者移除视觉到触觉的访问并独立处理每个未来模态。

Result: 在UniVTAC仿真基准的七个任务中，AFM达到32.0%的平均成功率，而IFM为23.7%，UniVTAC-ACT为14.9%。

Conclusion: 受控制的对比研究表明，选择性的相位对齐视觉-触觉路由提供了比完全模态隔离更具可操作性的未来到动作桥梁，证明了在动作专家获取未来信息时，跨模态相位对齐的访问结构比模态独立处理更有效。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation remains challenging because successful control depends on physical interaction cues that are often weakly observable from vision alone. Recent tactile world action models jointly model future visual observations and tactile signals to guide action generation, but how such futures should be structured for effective use by the action expert remains underexplored. Directly studying this question with learned world action models is difficult because end-to-end behavior entangles physically invalid visual futures, unreliable predictions, inaccurate or cross-modally inconsistent tactile forecasts, and an unreadable future-to-action interface. To make this interface independently studyable, we introduce Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF), a controlled framework that supplies paired RGB and tactile futures from successful trajectories verified in simulation. By fixing the future provider, OVTF isolates the interface and asks a cleaner question: if the future is successful and physically executable, what representation allows the action expert to absorb its benefit? Within OVTF, we propose Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM), in which visual memory reads future vision, each tactile memory jointly attends to its own tactile stream and phase-aligned future vision, and cross-tactile access is blocked. We compare AFM with Modality-Isolated Future Memory (IFM), which removes visual-to-tactile access and processes each future modality independently. Across seven tasks on the UniVTAC simulation benchmark, AFM achieves 32.0% average success, compared with 23.7% for IFM and 14.9% for UniVTAC-ACT. This controlled comparison shows that selective phase-aligned visual-tactile routing provides a more actionable future-to-action bridge than complete modality isolation.

</details>

*Xiaoyang Chen,Shengcheng Luo,Haoran Guo,Jiaming Jiang,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Dexterous object rotation is a sequential contact problem: each support, release, and re-contact decision must both produce the desired object motion, and prepare the hand configuration for continued rotation. Existing reinforcement learning methods discover such movement patterns through trial and error on specific robotic hand embodiments, without explicitly accounting for how each contact transition affects the hand's ability to sustain object rotation in subsequent steps. We introduce DexMani, a framework that transfers human demonstrations as contact-conditioned manipulability evolution. This prior captures how successful human contact transitions reshape the object-rotation directions available to the hand. DexMani then learns this manipulability evolution and uses it to guide downstream reinforcement learning, enabling rotation skills to be acquired across robot embodiments with distinct kinematics and active-contact configurations. Across the Shadow Hand, Allegro Hand, and XHand, DexMani achieves the highest success rates in every evaluated setting for both seen and unseen objects. DexMani reaches an average success rate of 57.5% on LEAP Hand, outperforming other baselines and producing smoother rotatory motions. Project site: https://dexmani.github.io

</details>


### [72] [Latency-Tolerant Cloud-Edge Collaborative Vision-Language-Action Models via Emergent Representational Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00569)
*Daojie Peng,Fulong Ma,Bingtao Wang,Sheng Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudEdgeVLA，一种云-边协同的VLA策略，将时间不对齐问题转化为表征学习问题。云端VLA将延迟的观测编码为缓慢变化的任务特征，轻量级边缘头将最新的云端特征与当前本地视觉相结合。在训练中，当前帧与随机延迟帧配对同一动作目标，鼓励云端表示保留任务级信息，边缘路径提供状态敏感修正。在四个LIBERO基准上，40步均匀延迟窗口下成功率保持63.8-78.0%，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动机器人上部署十亿参数的VLA策略存在系统冲突：语义推理受益于云端GPU，而闭环控制必须在本地快速响应，不能受网络延迟和抖动影响。现有的分层和异步策略虽提高了吞吐量，但慢路径表示可能过期到达或需要显式调度和延迟提示。

Method: CloudEdgeVLA采用云端-边缘分离的架构：云端VLA将延迟观测编码为缓慢变化的任务特征，轻量级边缘头结合最新的云端特征和当前本地视觉做出控制决策。训练时将当前帧和随机延迟帧与相同的当前动作目标配对，分别通过fresh path和stale path进行学习，目标是让云端表示保留任务级信息，边缘路径补充状态敏感修正，从而消除控制环中的阻塞同步。

Result: 在四个LIBERO基准上，40步均匀延迟窗口下CloudEdgeVLA保持63.8-78.0%的成功率，而VLASH最高仅6.4%，评估的单路径基线最高仅3.0%，展现了对网络延迟的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过去除控制环中的阻塞同步，CloudEdgeVLA为可扩展的VLA部署提供了实用路径：云端模型可以持续增长，而边缘计算保持轻量化和高响应性，实现了云端推理能力与本地实时控制需求的有效解耦。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies on mobile robots creates a systems conflict: semantic reasoning benefits from cloud GPUs, whereas closed-loop control must respond locally despite network delay and jitter. Existing hierarchical and asynchronous policies improve throughput, but their slow-path representations can still arrive stale or require explicit scheduling and delay cues. We introduce CloudEdgeVLA, a cloud-edge policy that treats temporal misalignment as a representation-learning problem. A cloud VLA encodes delayed observations into slowly varying task features, while a lightweight edge head combines the latest available cloud feature with current local vision. During training, current and randomly delayed frames are paired with the same current action target in fresh and stale paths. This objective encourages the cloud representation to preserve task-level information while the edge path supplies state-sensitive corrections. Across four LIBERO suites, CloudEdgeVLA retains 63.8--78.0% success with a 40-step uniform-delay window, whereas VLASH reaches at most 6.4% and the evaluated single-path baselines at most 3.0%. By removing blocking synchronization from the control loop, the design offers a practical route to scalable VLA deployment in which cloud models can grow while edge computation remains lightweight and responsive.

</details>


### [73] [Grasp Execution Without a Planner: Configuration-Space Grasp Distance Fields with Certified Safety & Guaranteed Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00600)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)，一种在臂-手配置空间中定义的光滑softmin距离场，用于直接通过负梯度反馈律执行抓取，无需规划器、存储轨迹或抓取点选择，并结合CBF-CLF二次规划滤除不安全指令，保证安全集前向不变，在仿真中成功抓取50个物体中的46个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的多指抓取执行架构需要先规划无碰撞轨迹到选定抓取姿态再用反馈律跟踪，但执行时的物体位姿不确定性或扰动可能使规划轨迹失效，导致代价高昂的重新规划。作者希望消除对规划器、存储轨迹和抓取点选择的依赖，实现鲁棒且无需重规划的抓取执行。

Method: 1) 构建Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)：在臂-手联合配置空间上定义一组候选抓取配置的softmin距离场，作为光滑的目标函数。2) 控制器沿该场负梯度方向执行固定反馈律，无需规划或轨迹存储。3) 安全性通过CBF-CLF二次规划(QP)滤波实现，约束自碰撞、工作空间、物体和障碍物间隙，并将受阻进度作为显式松弛变量输出。4) 由于光滑场无法刻画手-物接触切换，在前抓取构型处采用带滞回的模式切换，从物体碰撞避免切换到接触接收。5) 通过力矩质量障碍函数使已实现的抓取力闭合裕度保持在抓取起始时的规定容差内。

Result: 作者在固定基座机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人（均配备同样的欠驱动手）的动力学仿真中评估，控制器在杂乱和动态场景中成功抓取并举起50个物体中的46个（涵盖原始、家用和对抗性类别）。抓取中位保留94%的综合质量裕度，QP求解时间0.09ms/20ms控制步。逐步softmax权重证实控制器执行最近候选抓取，无需单独的选择步骤。

Conclusion: GDF提供了一种无需规划器、无轨迹存储、无抓取选择的端到端反馈式抓取执行方案，在存在位姿不确定性和动态障碍的场景下仍能可靠地安全抓取，并具有可证明的安全性和实时计算能力，为鲁棒抓取执行提供了新范式。

Abstract: Standard multifingered grasp execution architectures plan a collision-free trajectory to a selected grasp pose and track it with a feedback law. Execution-time object pose uncertainty or perturbations may invalidate the planned trajectory, forcing a costly replanning step. We present Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs), a smooth softmin distance field over grasp configurations in the arm-hand configuration space. Our controller executes a grasp by following this field's negative gradient with a stationary feedback law, eliminating the need for a planner, stored trajectory, or grasp selection. For safety, we filter this command through a CBF-CLF quadratic program (QP) constraining self-collision, workspace, object, and obstacle clearance, reporting impeded progress as explicit slack. We prove that the softmin tracks the true set distance within \(\log N/ρ\) for \(N\) candidates and smoothing parameter \(ρ\), and that the filtered loop renders the safe set forward invariant. Since no smooth field captures hand-object contact switching, we switch modes with hysteresis at a pregrasp configuration, trading object collision avoidance for contact admission. A wrench-quality barrier then keeps the realized grasp's force-closure margin within a prescribed tolerance of its value at hold onset. We evaluate in kinematic simulation on a fixed-base arm and a Unitree G1 humanoid, both fitted with the same underactuated hand. Our controller navigates cluttered and dynamic scenes to safely reach for, grasp, and lift 46 of 50 objects spanning primitive, household, and adversarial classes. Across those lifts, the executed grasps retain a median 94\% of their synthesized quality margin, at 0.09 ms QP solve time per 20 ms control step. Per-step softmax weights confirm that our controller executes the nearest candidate, obviating a separate selection step. Project page: www.clintonenwerem.com/gdf.

</details>


### [74] [From Failures to Supervision: DynamicEnvPlan for Robust Long-Horizon Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00613)
*Hao Yuan,Yuxin Wang,Lei Ji,Zhiwei Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynamicEnvPlan 是一个面向动态物理世界环境的闭环高层规划框架，通过规划、扰动和防护校正模块将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向的训练轨迹，并采用分阶段监督微调使规划器同时从正常执行和扰动恢复轨迹中学习。在104个任务-场景组合的实验中，将基础规划器的成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在全部七项评估指标（包括安全性和功能合规性）上取得改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界交互本质上是动态的，环境在执行过程中可能发生变化，要求智能体在非平稳条件下调整计划。现有具身任务基准虽然能暴露此类失败，但通常仅将失败视为评估结果而非可学习的训练信号，缺乏让智能体从失败中恢复的系统性训练机制。

Method: DynamicEnvPlan 采用闭环框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 具身任务执行扩展，使用人形智能体和高层基础技能；(2) 结构化语义记忆；(3) 可控扰动机制；(4) 由规划、扰动和防护校正模块组成的数据合成设计，将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向轨迹。随后通过分阶段监督微调，使规划器同时学习正常执行和扰动恢复两类轨迹。

Result: 在104个任务-场景组合（涵盖i.i.d.、组合泛化和分布外设置）上进行微调与评估，DynamicEnvPlan 将基础规划器成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在全部七项物理世界交互关键评估指标（包括安全性和功能合规性）上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 动态环境下的执行失败可以被转化为可学习的恢复信号，通过受控数据合成和监督微调，规划器能够有效学习在非平稳条件下的自适应恢复能力，显著提升物理世界交互的鲁棒性、安全性和泛化性能。

Abstract: Physical-world interaction is inherently dynamic, as environments can evolve during execution, requiring agents to adapt their plans under non-stationary conditions. We study this challenge through long-horizon embodied planning under environment deviations and execution uncertainty. Existing embodied-task benchmarks can expose such failures, but these failures are usually treated as evaluation outcomes instead of learnable signals for training agents to recover. In this work, we introduce DynamicEnvPlan, a closed-loop framework for high-level planning in dynamic environments. It extends embodied task execution with humanoid agents, high-level primitive skills, structured semantic memory, and controllable perturbations. Our data synthesis design consists of planning, perturbation, and guarded correction modules that turn dynamic execution states into recovery-oriented traces. The resulting traces are used for staged supervised fine-tuning, enabling the planner to learn from both nominal execution and perturbed recovery trajectories. Using 104 task-scene combinations spanning i.i.d., compositional generalization, and out-of-distribution settings for fine-tuning and evaluation, DynamicEnvPlan boosts success rate from 33.3% for the base planner to 76.2%, while improving across all seven evaluation metrics critical to physical-world interaction, including safety and affordance compliance.

</details>


### [75] [Learning-Based Motion Planning for Dynamic Environments: From Foundational Algorithms to Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00625)
*Zongyuan Shen,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Rui Cheng,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015至2025年间动态环境下的运动规划研究成果，提出了一种"学习角色"分类法，将现有方法按学习在规划管线中的参与方式分为直接策略学习、学习增强的经典规划、混合规划和训练增强方法四类，并系统分析了观察表示、预测不确定性、交互建模、规划器集成、安全约束和训练策略等关键因素，最后讨论了从仿真到现实迁移、安全可认证规划等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动规划是机器人领域的核心问题，广泛应用于自动驾驶、服务机器人等多个场景。然而现有的综述缺少对学习与经典规划方法之间交互关系的系统性梳理，需要一种能够按学习参与方式对方法进行分类的框架，以帮助研究者理解近年来学习驱动方法如何扩展、补充或与经典规划方法相互作用。

Method: 作者首先回顾经典规划方法作为算法基础和参考框架，然后提出"学习角色"（role-of-learning）分类法，将方法分为四类：直接策略学习、学习增强的经典规划、混合规划、训练增强方法。针对每类方法，文章总结了问题设定、代表性算法、关键思想、集成机制、优势和局限；进一步从观察表示、预测不确定性、交互建模、规划器集成、安全约束和训练策略六个维度分析了影响学习型运动规划设计的因素。

Result: 文章呈现了一个基于学习角色分类法的系统综述，覆盖了2015至2025年间的主要工作，对各方法类别的组成和特征进行了详细归纳，并从六个关键设计维度分析了影响方法效果的要素，识别了各类方法的优缺点。

Conclusion: 文章总结了现有学习型运动规划的进展和不足，指出了未来值得关注的方向，包括仿真到现实的差距（sim-to-real gap）、安全可认证规划、密集人群导航、感知-规划耦合以及具身AI等，强调需要将学习方法的灵活性与经典规划的可保证性相结合。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments is a fundamental problem in robotics, aiming to generate safe and efficient paths, trajectories, or control actions in the presence of moving obstacles, uncertain predictions, and multi-agent interactions. It has broad applications in autonomous driving, service robotics, warehouse logistics, human-robot collaboration, crowd navigation, and multi-robot systems. This survey reviews representative works published primarily between 2015 and 2025, with a particular focus on how recent learning-based advances extend, complement, or interact with classical planning foundations. We first revisit classical planning methods as algorithmic foundations and reference frameworks for learning-based extensions. We then propose a role-of-learning taxonomy that categorizes existing methods according to how learning participates in the planning pipeline, including direct policy learning, learning-augmented classical planning, hybrid planning, and training enhancement methods. For each category, we summarize the main problem settings, representative algorithms, key ideas, integration mechanisms, strengths, and limitations. We further analyze how observation representations, prediction uncertainty, interaction modeling, planner integration, safety constraints, and training strategies shape learning-based motion planning in dynamic environments. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including sim-to-real gap, safe and certifiable planning, dense crowd navigation, perception-planning coupling, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [76] [FlowPilot: Real-Time World-Action Modeling for Agile UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00635)
*Runqing Wang,Ding Yu,Pengyuan Min,Xinhong Zhang,Wei Xiao,Yu Hu,Jie Chen,Fu Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot是一个紧凑的世界-动作联合模型，通过流匹配联合去噪未来深度观测和可执行轨迹，实现无人机实时机载导航，在Jetson Orin NX上运行时间低于18ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机导航方案分为两类：map-then-optimize管线需要本地重建，计算开销大；端到端策略缺乏显式场景预测。本文旨在克服这两类方法的局限，在紧凑模型中同时实现未来场景预测和轨迹生成。

Method: FlowPilot采用双流混合Transformer架构，通过共享注意力耦合视频专家和动作专家，利用流匹配（flow matching）联合去噪未来深度观测与可执行轨迹。轨迹参数化为7阶Bernstein多项式，当前状态约束初始控制点，网络预测5个自由控制点，可得到C^2连续且具有闭合形式速度、加速度和急动度的参考。训练采用三级深度金字塔：高吞吐仿真、真实感仿真和真实机载数据。

Result: 在闭环仿真中，FlowPilot在逐渐增加的杂乱环境和最高8m/s的命令速度下优于基于学习和基于优化的基线。在实体四旋翼上，完整的感知到动作管线在Jetson Orin NX上运行时间低于18ms，在室内复杂环境和森林环境中仅依靠机载感知与计算达到5.5m/s的速度。

Conclusion: FlowPilot通过世界-动作联合建模有效平衡了预测能力与运行效率，证明了紧凑模型能够在真实机载平台上实现实时、安全的无人机自主导航。

Abstract: We present FlowPilot, a compact world-action model for real-time onboard UAV navigation from depth. Unlike map-then-optimize pipelines that require local reconstruction or end-to-end policies that lack explicit scene prediction, FlowPilot jointly denoises future depth observations and executable trajectories with flow matching. A dual-stream mixture-of-transformers couples video and action experts through shared attention, allowing future-scene prediction and trajectory generation to inform each other. At deployment, the model runs action-centrically and outputs only a trajectory. To ensure trackability, actions are parameterized as degree-7 Bernstein polynomials: the current state constrains the initial control points, and the network predicts five free control points, yielding C^2-continuous references with closed-form velocity, acceleration and jerk. FlowPilot is trained on a three-level depth pyramid spanning high-throughput simulation, photorealistic simulation, and real onboard data. In closed-loop simulation, it outperforms learning- and optimization-based baselines under increasing clutter and commanded speeds up to 8m/s. On a physical quadrotor, the full perception-to-action pipeline runs in under 18ms on a Jetson Orin NX and reaches 5.5m/s in cluttered indoor and forest environments using only onboard sensing and computation.

</details>


### [77] [Assistant Placement Aria: A Benchmark for Egocentric Placement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00652)
*Amir Belder,Gonçalo Dias Pais,Refael Vivanti,Omri Carmi,Daniel DeTone,Oren Shrout,Ido Gattegno,Ayellet Tal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Assistant Placement Aria，这是首个用于虚拟放置（VP）任务的基准数据集，涵盖全局、局部和人类中心约束，包含合成与真实室内场景，支持2D面板放置、就坐建议和电视放置三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助中的虚拟放置（VP）任务要求识别场景中所有符合人类意图的目标位置，而非传统的单一预定义位置。VP问题涉及场景几何、语义和合理性的全局与局部推理，具有高度复杂性，但该领域缺乏系统性的基准数据来推动研究。

Method: 构建了Assistant Placement Aria基准，包含合成和真实室内场景，每场景提供2D图像、3D点云和场景对象文本描述。定义了三个标注任务：2D面板放置、就坐建议、电视放置，覆盖全局、局部和人类中心约束。

Result: 在基准上评估了多种基础模型的对象检测和分割性能，为后续研究提供了基线参考。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了虚拟放置领域数据匮乏的空白，旨在激励更多研究关注这一依赖数据且具有挑战性的方向。

Abstract: Human assistance in robotics spans around several tasks such as navigation, object manipulation, and placement, where a key challenge is selecting target destinations that align with human intentions or preferences. We focus on this challenge in the context of Virtual Placement (VP), the task of identifying all plausible target locations given scene context and human-centric constraints. This differs from traditional placement tasks that typically focus on a single, predefined target location. The VP problem is complex, as it requires both global and local reasoning about the scene's geometry, semantics, and plausibility. To address this gap, we introduce {\bf Assistant Placement Aria}, the first benchmark to explore diverse aspects of VP, including global, local, and human-centric constraints. It contains both synthetic and real indoor scenes annotated for three tasks: (i)~2D Panel Placement, (ii)~Sitting Suggestion, and (iii)~TV Placement. Each scene includes 2D images, a 3D point cloud, and a textual description of the objects within the scene. By contributing this benchmark, we aim to encourage further research in this underexplored and challenging field that is critically dependent on relevant data. We also evaluate several foundation models for object detection and segmentation on our benchmark.

</details>


### [78] [Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT) for Hip Exoskeletons: Metabolic and Biomechanical Validation of a Simulation-Trained Co-Adaptive Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00715)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次对"分级多智能体训练（SMAT）"方法进行了生理学验证：在纯物理仿真中训练的外骨骼控制策略，直接部署到真实髋关节外骨骼上，对8名健康成年人的测试显示，主动辅助相比被动状态将净代谢率降低了19.7%（p<0.001），且在不同行走速度和地形上均表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制器可在纯物理仿真中训练外骨骼辅助策略，但很少有控制器能解决人机协同适应问题，并通过全身代谢测量这一金标准在真实用户上得到验证。协同适应具有挑战性：设备改变关节动力学时，穿戴者会重组神经肌肉协调，形成非平稳学习问题。

Method: 采用SMAT四阶段课程，逐步训练一个肌肉骨骼人体智能体和一个双侧髋关节外骨骼智能体。将策略部署到髋关节外骨骼上，通过间接测热法测量8名健康成年人在无外骨骼、被动和主动三种条件下的代谢成本，并进行生物力学分析。

Result: 主动辅助相比被动设备使净代谢率降低19.7%（p<0.001）；所有受试者的髋关节正功功率比中位数为0.98，证实以正机械功率为主；策略在不同行走速度和地形上均能泛化。

Conclusion: 单一仿真训练的SMAT策略无需受试者特定再训练，即可在真实用户上带来显著的代谢收益，并在超出训练条件的场景中保持稳健性。

Abstract: Learning-based controllers can deliver exoskeleton assistance after training entirely in physics-based simulation, yet few controllers that address human-device co-adaptation have been validated on real users by whole-body metabolic measurement, the standard benchmark for assistive walking. Co-adaptation is challenging: as the device alters joint dynamics, the wearer reorganizes neuromuscular coordination, producing a non-stationary learning problem. Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT), a four-stage curriculum that progressively trains a musculoskeletal human actor and a bilateral hip exoskeleton actor, was introduced and shown to reduce simulated hip-muscle activation and provide positive assistance on hardware. This article provides the first physiological validation of SMAT. The policy was deployed on a hip exoskeleton and tested with eight healthy adults, with metabolic cost measured by indirect calorimetry across no-exoskeleton, passive, and active conditions. Active assistance lowered net metabolic rate by 19.7% relative to the passive device (p < 0.001). Biomechanical analysis confirmed predominantly positive hip mechanical power across all subjects (positive-power ratio 0.98), and the policy generalized across walking speeds and terrains. Together, these results show that a single simulation-trained SMAT policy, deployed without subject-specific retraining, delivers a significant metabolic benefit on real users while remaining robust beyond the conditions it was trained on.

</details>


### [79] [OmniAI: A Surface-Adaptive Aerial Projection Interface for Human--Drone Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00721)
*Nikita Kuzmin,Yuhua Jin,Georgii Demianchuk,Mariya Lezina,Fawad Mehboob,Ivan Valuev,Nikolai Lutsenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAI是一种体航空代理（无人机），通过机载屏幕与附近环境表面之间的投影切换实现表面自适应交互。它利用伺服驱动的MEMS激光投影仪，通过RGB-D感知和RANSAC平面拟合在线检测投影表面，并整合语音和空中手势控制，构建面向情境感知人机交互的移动空间AR界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在人类环境中缺乏具备空间依据的交互界面，传统无人机通信方式（如屏幕或单一投影）难以在动态环境中提供情境化、可适应的信息呈现，需要一种能够根据环境表面灵活切换投影方式的交互接口。

Method: OmniAI采用伺服驱动的MEMS激光投影系统，可在机载屏幕和周围环境表面（如墙壁）之间切换投影；通过RGB-D感知和RANSAC平面拟合在线检测可用投影表面，无需预建地图；使用Web增强的LLM流水线生成文本和图像响应；同时提供功能等价的语音和空中手势控制，用于无人机运动和投影内容操作。

Result: 该论文展示了OmniAI将语音、空中手势、自适应投影和航空机动性相结合的能力，实现了面向情境感知人机交互的移动空间增强现实（AR）界面，使无人机能够在动态环境中提供具备空间上下文的视觉信息。

Conclusion: OmniAI证明了无人机可以作为移动空间AR界面，通过表面自适应的动态投影实现情境感知的人机交互，为无人机在人类日常环境中的空间通信提供了新范式。

Abstract: Drones in human environments often lack spatially grounded in- terfaces for situated communication. We present OmniAI, an em- bodied aerial agent that supports surface-adaptive interaction by switching projection between an onboard screen and nearby en- vironmental surfaces. A servo-actuated MEMS laser projector renders text-and-image responses from a web-augmented LLM pipeline. Projection surfaces are detected online using RGB-D sensing and RANSAC plane fitting, without pre-mapped geometry. OmniAI provides functionally equivalent voice and gesture con- trol for both drone motion and projected content. By combining speech, mid-air gestures, adaptive projection, and aerial mobility, OmniAI demonstrates a mobile spatial AR interface for context- aware human-drone interaction.

</details>


### [80] [SelfWAM: A Self-Grounded Unified World Action Model for Fast Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00725)
*Bikang Pan,Fan Liu,Haotao Lu,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SelfWAM是一个基于模态专精Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构的统一自接地世界动作模型，通过同时预测动作、动作条件化的未来RGB帧和机器人自掩码，将未来预测与执行动作的后果紧密绑定，从而在保持快速策略推理的同时提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）仅以任务提示和观测上下文为条件进行未来预测，可能导致模型捕获任务的一般性进展而非动作特有的后果。为了让未来预测真正反映执行动作带来的具体影响，需要将预测与动作及其对机器人自身可见运动的影响联系起来。

Method: SelfWAM采用模态专精的Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，在联合训练中实现三方面预测：动作、动作条件化的未来RGB帧和机器人自掩码。其核心机制包括：(1) 干净动作条件化——未来视觉查询可以关注演示动作的干净副本，使视频分支成为动作特异性的后果模型，同时保持仅动作推断的快速路径不变；(2) 提示专用的未来机器人自掩码预测目标——去除外观细节，提供与条件化动作时间演化紧密耦合的训练目标，将视频学习聚焦于动作相关的视觉变化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，SelfWAM能生成更具动作敏感性的未来预测，同时保持快速策略推理能力，并显著提升策略性能。

Conclusion: 通过干净动作条件化和未来自掩码监督的双重机制，SelfWAM使未来预测更直接地反映执行动作如何改变机器人自身的可见运动及周围场景，验证了自接地未来预测对提升机器人策略学习的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling actions and future observations. However, conditioning future prediction only on the task prompt and observation context risks capturing generic task progression rather than the action-specific consequences of the executed action. We introduce SelfWAM, a unified self-grounded WAM built on a modality-specialized Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture that jointly predicts actions, action-conditioned future RGB frames, and robot self-masks, thereby grounding future prediction in the robot's visible body and its action-induced motion. During joint training, SelfWAM allows future visual queries to attend to a clean copy of the demonstrated action, turning the video branch into an action-specific consequence model while leaving the fast action-only inference path unchanged. To focus video learning on action-relevant visual changes, we use prompt-specific objectives for future robot self-mask prediction, which removes appearance details and provides a target whose temporal evolution is tightly coupled with the conditioning action. Together, clean-action conditioning and future self-mask supervision make future predictions more directly reflect how the executed action changes the robot's visible motion and the surrounding scene. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that SelfWAM produces more action-sensitive futures and preserves fast policy inference, while improving policy performance.

</details>


### [81] [Push-Wiper: Toward General-Purpose Robotic Cleaning across Varied Stains and Surfaces with Segmented Pushing Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00730)
*Renhao Lu,Mingxin Wang,Chenyang Cao,Yang Yang,Guoping Pan,Kangkang Dong,Yi Cheng,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Push-Wiper框架，将粘性污渍清洁重新定义为聚合问题，通过海绵分段推动轨迹逐步聚集污渍，并结合后处理实现海绵自清洁，在清洁分数上较基线提升高达130%，且零样本泛化到多种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性污渍因高粘度和复杂流变特性而成为机器人表面清洁的重大挑战；传统擦拭会扩散污渍，而刷洗虽摩擦力强但易损伤表面。

Method: 采用分段推动轨迹利用海绵逐步聚集污渍（聚合阶段），随后通过后处理阶段分离聚集物并实现海绵自清洁；污渍聚集采用逐步策略，利用Diffusion Policy生成自适应推动动作序列；动作执行依赖Arbitrary Surface Pose Interpolator (ASPI)与混合力-位控制器，实现对不同空间分布污渍的泛化。

Result: 在清洁分数（污渍去除面积百分比）上较基线方法提升高达130%；无需额外训练即可零样本迁移至固体残留、液体溢出、未见粘性污渍以及不同曲率的曲面。

Conclusion: 实验证明了Push-Wiper在粘性污渍清洁中的有效性及强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Viscous stains, characterized by high viscosity and complex rheological properties, remain a major challenge for robotic surface cleaning. Conventional wiping often spreads the stain, while scrubbing provides stronger friction but risks damaging the surface. In this paper, we propose Push-Wiper, a framework that reformulates viscous stain cleaning as an aggregation problem. Push-Wiper employs a sponge to progressively gather stains through segmented pushing trajectories, followed by a post-processing phase that detaches the aggregated material and enables sponge self-cleaning. We adopt a stepwise strategy for stain gathering and leverage Diffusion Policy to generate adaptive pushing action sequences. These sequences are executed through our Arbitrary Surface Pose Interpolator (ASPI) and a hybrid force-position controller, allowing the method to generalize to stains with diverse spatial distributions. Push-Wiper achieves a cleaning score (CS), defined as the percentage of stain area removed, up to 130% higher than baseline methods. Without additional training, Push-Wiper also transfers in a zero-shot manner to solid residues, liquid spills, unseen viscous stains, and curved surfaces with varying geometries. Our experiments demonstrate the cleaning effectiveness of Push-Wiper and its strong generalization ability. The project website is available at https://push-wiper.github.io/.

</details>


### [82] [When Prompts Control Robots: Prompt Injection Attacks in Multi-Agent Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00747)
*Neha Nagaraja,Amisha Bagari,Hayretdin Bahsi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了多智能体LLM机器人系统中的提示注入攻击，评估了直接注入（任务指令）和间接注入（感知模块）两种攻击路径，并分析了攻击在智能体间传播的机制以及架构变化对攻击成功率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被集成到自主机器人系统中进行任务规划和控制，提示注入攻击可能导致不安全的决策和物理危害。多智能体设置通过跨智能体污染和更广的攻击面进一步放大了风险，但目前缺乏对此类攻击的系统性研究。

Method: 针对基于LLM的多智能体机器人系统，从两个维度设计攻击：直接注入（任务指令）和间接注入（感知模块）；在单智能体和多智能体场景下，跨不同攻击目标复杂度和注入策略进行实验，并分析架构变化对LLM查询及攻击成功的影响。

Result: 实验表明提示注入能够诱导对抗性行为并降低任务完成率；攻击可通过共享提示结构在智能体间传播，其影响因提示组成和目标智能体而异；架构变化会改变LLM查询方式，从而影响攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究多智能体LLM机器人系统中提示注入攻击的工作，揭示了此类系统在安全性方面的脆弱性，为后续防御研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly integrated into autonomous robotic systems for task planning and control, but this integration exposes them to prompt injection attacks that can lead to unsafe decisions and physical harm. Multi-agent settings increase the risks through cross-agent contamination and broader attack surfaces. In this paper, we evaluate prompt injection attacks against an LLM-based multi-agent robotic system, considering both direct injections into task instructions and indirect injections through perception modules. In our experiments across varying attack-goal complexities and injection strategies in both single-agent and multi-agent settings, we show that prompt injection can induce adversarial actions while reducing task completion. We find that attacks can propagate from one agent to others through shared prompt structures, with impacts varying depending on prompt composition and the targeted agent. We further analyze how architectural changes affect LLM queries and, consequently, the attack success. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study that systematically investigates prompt injection attacks in a multi-agent LLM-based robotic system.

</details>


### [83] [ORCESTRA: VLM-driven Visual Robot programming in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00775)
*Ivan Snegirev,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Mikhail Konenkov,Artem Lykov,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ORCESTRA是一个混合现实系统，通过无代码航点教学和语言引导控制来编程机器人数字孪生，支持异构机器人形态，并利用MR验证作为语言引导机器人编程的安全层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要专业知识，而语言引导的编程方式在物理部署前缺乏安全验证机制。作者旨在通过混合现实中的数字孪生技术，提供一种直观、安全且支持异构机器人的编程方案。

Method: 系统在透视式混合现实工作空间中，用户可在真实表面上放置机器人孪生体，教授轨迹、保存机器人相对坐标系下的片段，或通过语音/文本命令由视觉语言模型转换为结构化的数字孪生计划。两种交互模式共享同一后端，包含度量接地、具身感知验证、预览、确认及数字孪生执行模块。

Result: 系统支持固定基座机械臂、移动底座和人形机器人等多种异构机器人形态，验证了MR验证作为语言引导机器人编程在物理部署前的安全层的有效性。

Conclusion: ORCESTRA证明了混合现实数字孪生结合无代码教学与语言引导控制，能够为异构机器人提供安全、直观的编程体验，MR验证可作为物理部署前的关键安全屏障。

Abstract: ORCESTRA is a mixed-reality system for programming robot digital twins through no-code waypoint teaching and language-guided control. In a passthrough mixed-reality workspace, users place robot twins on real surfaces, teach trajectories, save robot-relative episodes, or issue spoken/typed commands that a vision-language model converts into structured digital-twin plans. Both interaction modes share a backend for metric grounding, embodiment-aware validation, preview, confirmation, and digital-twin execution. The system supports heterogeneous robot embodiments, including fixed-base manipulators, a mobile base, and a humanoid robot, demonstrating MR validation as a safety layer for language-guided robot programming before physical deployment.

</details>


### [84] [SIPTraj: Map-Free End-to-End Trajectory Prediction via Physics-Guided Scene Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00779)
*Feifei Liu,Zejun Wei,Haozhe Wang,Yazhi Ye,Yuying Zhang,Jintao Cheng,Chi Man Vong,Xieyuanli Chen,Xiaoyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIPTraj是一个无高精地图的轨迹预测框架，通过层级化智能体-场景编码器（HASE）实现场景锚定，并利用物理引导的迭代解码器（PGID）将运动学约束内化到模型表示中，在nuScenes和Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集上超越了现有无地图方法和强地图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无高精地图条件下，传感器导出的BEV特征缺乏显式车道拓扑和可行驶区域先验，难以将每个智能体锚定到场景上下文中；同时纯数据驱动学习难以显式编码运动学约束和物理可行性。现有无地图预测器通过单一融合步骤以智能体无关方式提取场景上下文，并将物理约束仅作为输出层罚项处理，两种挑战均未得到解决。

Method: SIPTraj包含两大核心模块：(1) 层级化智能体-场景编码器（HASE），通过智能体引导的场景证据逐步锚定每个智能体，并在场景锚定空间中细化智能体间关系；(2) 物理引导的迭代解码器（PGID），以瞬时运动学状态为解码条件，将物理监督传播到内部表示而非仅作用于输出轨迹。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集上的大量实验表明，SIPTraj在推理时不依赖任何高精地图，超越了先前的无地图预测器以及强地图基线的性能。

Conclusion: SIPTraj证明了无需高精地图即可同时解决场景锚定和物理可行性的问题，且性能优于依赖地图的方法；代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction of surrounding agents is a prerequisite for safe planning and decision making in autonomous driving. Without high-definition (HD) maps, sensor-derived bird's-eye-view (BEV) features provide no explicit lane topology or drivable-area priors, making it inherently difficult to ground each agent in its surrounding scene context. Moreover, physical feasibility remains difficult to capture through data-driven learning alone, as kinematic constraints on agent motion cannot be explicitly encoded without structured supervision. Existing map-free predictors extract scene context in an agent-agnostic manner through a single fusion step and treat physical constraints only as output-level penalties, leaving both challenges unaddressed. We propose SIPTraj, a map-free trajectory prediction framework that jointly addresses scene grounding and physical feasibility. SIPTraj introduces a Hierarchical Agent-Scene Encoder (HASE) progressively grounding each agent in agent-guided scene evidence and refining inter-agent relations within the scene-grounded space. To tackle physical infeasibility in predicted trajectories, we develop a Physics-Guided Iterative Decoder (PGID). It conditions decoding on instantaneous kinematic states, propagating physical supervision into internal representations rather than output trajectories alone. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 Sensor show that SIPTraj surpasses prior map-free predictors and strong map-based baselines without any HD map at inference. Our code will be released as open-source.

</details>


### [85] [StochSIPP: Safe Interval Path Planning in Stochastic Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00792)
*Ajith Kemisetti,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StochSIPP，一个在时间依赖且不确定的路径图上进行精确条件规划的方法。它结合SIPP生成的认证安全宏动作与有界AND/OR搜索，在局部观测边/顶点状态的情况下选择动作，提供无碰撞保证并最小化期望到达时间。实验表明相比保守固定路径规划，它在保持安全性的同时减少到达时间，并能解决门控场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定的时间依赖阻塞环境下进行安全导航，需要在执行运动前预见观测结果。现有保守固定路径规划方法在面对门控场景（需根据观测结果决策）时无法返回任何可行计划，因此需要一种能显式考虑观测并据此调整策略的条件规划方法。

Method: StochSIPP包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用SIPP生成认证安全的宏动作，这些宏动作在执行过程中终止于下一个观测点或目标；(2) 在缓存的动作-观测图上进行有界AND/OR搜索，为每个可达的观测结果选择动作；(3) 采用乐观和稳健的SIPP松弛来为有界AND/OR搜索提供可采纳的上下界。

Result: 在受控路径图实例上，StochSIPP保持了安全固定路径基线的成功表现，同时减少了到达时间；并能解决保守固定路径规划器无法返回计划的门控场景。扩展性研究发现随着同时观测的不确定状态数量增加，计算时间快速增长。

Conclusion: 当每个被声明为确定性安全的区间真实安全、传感精确且执行遵循计划时间时，StochSIPP生成的策略是无可碰撞保证的；在正确的独立概率及完整的动作和结果生成的条件下，它在指定的路径图和水平范围内最小化期望到达时间。

Abstract: Safe navigation under uncertain time-dependent blockage requires anticipating observations before committing to motion. We present StochSIPP, an exact contingent planner for temporal roadmaps with uncertain edge and vertex statuses revealed locally during execution. StochSIPP uses SIPP to generate certified-safe macro-actions that terminate at the next observation or the goal, and bounded AND/OR search over a cached action--observation graph to select actions for every reachable observation outcome. Optimistic and robust SIPP relaxations provide admissible lower and upper bounds for bounded AND/OR search. When every interval declared deterministically safe is truly safe, sensing is exact, and execution follows the planned timing, the resulting policy is provably collision-free. With correct independent probabilities and complete action and outcome generation, it minimizes expected arrival time within the roadmap and horizon. Experiments on controlled roadmap instances show that StochSIPP preserves the observed success of safe fixed-path baselines while reducing arrival time, and solves gated scenarios in which conservative fixed-path planners return no plan. A scalability study further reveals rapid growth as the number of simultaneously observed uncertain statuses increases.

</details>


### [86] [DynamicWAM: Dual-Path Motion Conditioning for World-Action Models in Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00793)
*Yunfan Lou,Hewen Gao,Xiyu Zhu,Zhuoran Qiao,Xuan Han,Yifan Yang,Yifan Ye,Boxian Yao,Zhibo Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynamicWAM，一种用于动态物体操作的双路径运动条件紧凑型世界-动作模型（WAM），通过历史光流和运动学描述子分别条件化视觉与动作路径，结合联合注意力、蒸馏紧凑主干和基于实时分块的异步执行，实现动态场景下的响应式控制，在DOMINO基准和真实世界任务上均显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）通常仅以当前帧为条件，并同步执行大型骨干网络，缺乏对目标运动的感知能力和动态场景下的响应式控制能力，难以满足动态操作中对目标运动推断和及时响应的需求。

Method: DynamicWAM包含两条互补的运动条件路径：1）历史流条件化，利用冻结的预训练视频VAE对时间对齐的光流帧与当前观测进行联合编码，保留空间运动结构；2）运动学描述子注入，将位移、持续时间、速度和加速度等运动学信息注入动作专家，提供运动幅度与时序信息。两条路径通过联合世界-动作注意力进行融合，并采用蒸馏后的紧凑骨干和基于实时分块（RTC）的异步执行实现响应式控制。

Result: 在DOMINO基准上，DynamicWAM取得38.2%的成功率和53.2的操作分数，优于所有评估基线；在涵盖线性、圆形和复合目标运动的12个真实世界任务中，平均成功率达46.7%，比最强基线高出22.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过双路径运动条件的设计，DynamicWAM同时捕获了空间运动结构和运动量级与时序信息，结合紧凑骨干和异步执行，在动态物体操作任务上实现了显著优于现有方法的成功率，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Dynamic manipulation requires robots to infer target motion and respond promptly, yet existing World-Action Models (WAMs) typically condition only on the current frame and execute large backbones synchronously, limiting motion awareness and responsive control in dynamic scenes. We propose DynamicWAM, a compact WAM for dynamic object manipulation with dual-path motion conditioning. DynamicWAM introduces history-flow conditioning, encoding temporally aligned optical-flow frames alongside the current observation through a frozen pretrained video VAE to preserve spatial motion structure, while injecting kinematic descriptors of displacement, duration, velocity, and acceleration into the action expert to provide motion magnitude and timing. The two complementary paths are fused through joint world-action attention. A distilled compact backbone and real-time chunking (RTC)-based asynchronous execution further enable responsive control. On DOMINO, DynamicWAM achieves a 38.2% success rate and a 53.2 manipulation score, outperforming all evaluated baselines. Across 12 real-world tasks spanning linear, circular, and compound target motion, it achieves a 46.7% average success rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 22.9 percentage points.

</details>


### [87] [LooperMuscle: Fast and Stable Learning of Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking via Structured Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00820)
*Boyi Liu,Qijin Li,Tianqi Yu,Qinrui Yan,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: FastSAC-style methods significantly reduce humanoid motion training time but often suffer from notable performance degradation compared with PPO in whole-body tracking tasks. We target this speed-performance gap by introducing LooperMuscle, a composed expert policy learning framework that restores tracking quality while preserving high training efficiency. LooperMuscle combines a semantically structured mixture-of-experts actor, an expert-aware distributional critic, and contribution-routed replay with deferred curriculum scheduling. These three components form a closed training loop in which expert contributions guide data routing, routed data shape value learning, and value gradients in turn refine expert specialization. Empirically, our approach substantially outperforms vanilla FastSAC in motion tracking accuracy while requiring far less wall-clock time than PPO: where FastSAC trains in about 15 minutes but underperforms, and PPO achieves stronger results but requires about 6 hours, LooperMuscle recovers a substantial fraction of the remaining gap to PPO in roughly 45 minutes of simulation training, delivering practical efficiency for rapid policy iteration. The code will be released to benefit the research community at https://loopermuscle.github.io/.

</details>


### [88] [RIT*: Riemannian Informed Trees for Cost-Adaptive Optimal Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00822)
*Muhayy Ud Din,Ahmed Nadar,Jan Rosell,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*)，一种将批量知情搜索中的欧几里得基元替换为黎曼对应物的规划框架。RIT*构建更紧凑且代价一致的知情集，在各向异性距离度量下执行最近邻搜索，并通过级联方案高效评估边代价。同时引入碰撞自适应度量细化(CARM)，从碰撞反馈中在线学习障碍邻近代价场，减少对先验度量设计的依赖。实验覆盖2-D至14-D环境，显示在高维变度量空间中获得显著更低的代价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于欧几里得度量的知情规划算法在高维配置空间及度量随空间变化时性能退化，需要一种能够自适应捕捉空间各向异性与障碍邻近信息的规划框架。

Method: RIT*框架：(1)在批量知情搜索中用黎曼基元替代欧几里得基元；(2)构建更紧凑、代价一致的知情集；(3)在各向异性距离度量下进行最近邻搜索；(4)通过级联方案高效评估边代价。此外引入CARM模块，从碰撞反馈在线学习障碍邻近代价场，自适应细化度量。

Result: 在2-D至14-D环境中验证：低维及空间常量度量场景下与现有方法竞争力相当；高维变度量场景下显著降低解代价。相比BIT*，3-D各向异性基准中初始代价中位数提升13.0%，6-DOF操作中最终代价中位数提升9.0%，14-DOF双臂规划问题中提升24.8-63.5%。性能增益随各向异性和维度增大而扩展。

Conclusion: RIT*结合黎曼几何与自适应度量学习，有效解决高维各向异性配置空间中的运动规划问题，填补了欧几里得知情搜索在非欧几里得度量环境下的性能缺口。

Abstract: We present Riemannian Informed Trees (RIT*), a planning framework that replaces Euclidean primitives in batch-informed search with their Riemannian counterparts. RIT* constructs a tighter, cost-consistent informed set, performs a nearest-neighbour search under an anisotropic distance metric, and evaluates edge costs efficiently via a cascading scheme. We further introduce a Collision-Adaptive Metric Refinement (CARM), which learns an obstacle-proximity cost field online from collision feedback, reducing the reliance on prior metric design in practical settings. Experiments across environments from 2-D to 14-D show that RIT* is competitive in low-dimensional and spatially constant-metric settings and produces substantially lower-cost solutions when the metric varies spatially in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Performance gains scale with anisotropy and dimension, reaching up to 13.0% improvement in median initial cost over BIT* in the 3-D anisotropic benchmark, up to 9.0% in median final cost over BIT* in 6-DOF manipulation, and 24.8-63.5% in a 14-DOF bimanual planning problem, where Euclidean-informed baselines degrade. Videos and code can be found here: https://muhayyuddin.github.io/ritstar/

</details>


### [89] [GeminiPainter's sequence-formed pipeline comprised of perception, cognition, planning, and action stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00829)
*Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Aleksey Fedoseev,Iana Zhura,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自主机器人肖像生成系统，融合实时人脸检测、基于Gemini Vision API的AI素描生成和6自由度协作机械臂绘制，用户评分显示素描质量4.33、执行感知4.53、用户体验4.65，表明系统能生成可辨识、吸引人的机器人肖像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索计算机视觉、神经艺术抽象、运动优化与机器人控制的集成，实现从感知到认知再到行动的完整自主机器人艺术创作流水线。

Method: 系统采用感知-认知-行动流水线：(1) 实时捕获视频帧并提取人脸区域（感知）；(2) 使用Gemini Vision API将人脸转换为极简单线素描（认知）；(3) 通过基于图的路径规划优化笔画顺序；(4) 在6自由度协作机械臂上执行平滑轨迹（行动）。

Result: 在5分量表上用户评价较高：素描质量4.33、执行感知4.53、用户体验4.65，表明所生成的机器人肖像具有可辨识性、吸引力和参与感。

Conclusion: 该自主机器人肖像生成系统有效整合了多模态AI与机器人控制，能够生成高质量、受用户欢迎的肖像作品，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present an autonomous robotic portrait-generation system combining real-time face detection, AI-based sketch generation, and robotic drawing. The system captures video frames, extracts facial regions, converts them into minimalist single-line sketches using the Gemini Vision API, optimizes stroke order through graph-based path planning, and executes smooth trajectories on a 6-DoF collaborative manipulator. This perception-cognition-action pipeline integrates computer vision, neural artistic abstraction, motion optimization, and robot control. User ratings on a 5-point scale were high for sketch quality 4.33, perceived execution 4.53, and user experience 4.65, indicating recognizable, appealing, and engaging robotic portraits.

</details>


### [90] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Parallel-Link Leg Mechanisms via Simulator-Side Dynamics Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01697)
*Jinsong Hong,Jangho Kim,Jihwan Lee,Donghyun Kim,Sehoon Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Simulator-Side System Normalization (S3N)方法，解决并联机构在串行树仿真代理中因动力学重构导致的sim-to-real差距问题。S3N-Act通过坐标变换将执行器惯量和阻尼纳入串行坐标动力学，S3N-Full进一步通过分别辨识执行器和连杆级频率响应来恢复残余连杆惯量。在2-DoF验证中，S3N-Full将关节位置和扭矩RMSE分别降低80.9%和82.1%；在姿态保持和圆形行走中，地面反作用力RMSE和sim-to-real差距也显著减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机构在仿真中通常被简化为串行树代理表示，传统的基于Jacobian的状态和扭矩映射虽然保持了运动学和虚功关系的一致性，但忽略了坐标变换引起的执行器惯量与阻尼重新分布以及串行树简化过程中省略的连杆惯量，导致动力学层面的sim-to-real差距显著。

Method: 提出S3N框架，在保持串行树拓扑结构的前提下标准化其有效动力学。具体包含两个变体：(1) S3N-Act——通过坐标变换将执行器惯量和阻尼纳入串行坐标动力学；(2) S3N-Full——通过分别辨识执行器级和连杆级频率响应来恢复残余连杆惯量。

Result: 在2-DoF验证中，S3N-Full相对于Jacobian映射基线将关节位置RMSE降低80.9%、扭矩RMSE降低82.1%。在俯仰原地运动时，S3N-Act和S3N-Full分别将地面反作用力范数RMSE降低65.1%和62.4%。在圆形行走中，S3N-Full将相位平均、指令归一化的sim-to-real差距从17.3%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: 模拟器侧标准化能够在运动和力两个层面显著提升sim-to-real一致性，使得在串行树框架中进行策略训练时能够获得与真实并联机构更一致的硬件动力学，从而改善策略迁移效果。

Abstract: This paper addresses the sim-to-real gap in dynamics arising when a parallel-link mechanism is represented by a serial-tree surrogate in simulation. Conventional Jacobian-based state and torque mappings preserve consistency with the kinematic and virtual-work relations but do not account for the coordinate-induced redistribution of actuator inertia and damping and the linkage inertia omitted during serial-tree reduction. To address this gap, Simulator-Side System Normalization (S3N) is proposed to normalize the serial-tree simulator's effective dynamics while preserving its tree topology. S3N-Act incorporates actuator inertia and damping into the serial-coordinate dynamics through coordinate transformation, whereas S3N-Full restores residual linkage inertia by separately identifying actuator- and leg-level frequency responses. In the 2-DoF validation, S3N-Full reduced the joint-position and torque RMSEs by 80.9% and 82.1%, respectively, relative to the Jacobian-mapping baseline. During pitch-in-place motion, S3N-Act and S3N-Full reduced the RMSE of the ground reaction force norm by 65.1% and 62.4%, respectively. During circular locomotion, S3N-Full reduced the phase-averaged, command-normalized sim-to-real gap from 17.3% to 9.9%. These results show that simulator-side normalization improves motion- and force-level sim-to-real consistency. It enables policy training in a serial-tree framework with hardware-consistent dynamics that better represent the physical parallel-link mechanism.

</details>


### [91] [Minute-Scale Training for Microrobot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00854)
*Yinghan Sun,Aoji Zhu,Xiang Ji,Yamei Li,Jiachi Zhao,Yun Wang,Li Zhang,Huijun Gao,Lidong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的微型机器人导航学习框架，通过构建包含10,000多个虚拟血管环境的全向量化模拟器（每秒约190,000次状态转换），并结合任务塑形-正则化（TSR）奖励框架，将微型机器人导航策略的训练时间从数小时/天缩短至10分钟以内，同时支持跨微型机器人类型和导航场景的零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DRL微型机器人导航方法学习效率和有效性不足，模型训练需要数小时甚至数天，严重阻碍了快速实际部署和参数优化，亟需一种能够快速高效训练导航策略的方法。

Method: 提出了一个两部分的深度学习框架：（1）构建全向量化的模拟器，包含超过10,000个虚拟血管环境，并行化动力学仿真、LiDAR启发式感知和可行性检查，达到约190,000次转换/秒的速度；（2）提出任务塑形-正则化（TSR）奖励框架，通过加速收敛、提升最终性能来增强训练有效性。

Result: 训练时间缩短至10分钟以内；TSR框架在所有测试场景中减少动作变异至少33.7%，增加障碍物间隙至少2.1%；支持不同微型机器人类型和导航场景下的零样本部署。

Conclusion: 该框架显著缩短了设计循环，加速了自主微型机器人的实际部署，为微型机器人导航领域的快速训练和有效策略学习提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Microrobots hold significant potential for various applications, where targeted navigation is a basic requirement. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for fully autonomous microrobot navigation. Yet, current DRL-based approaches pay limited attention to learning efficiency and effectiveness, requiring hours to days for model training. Consequently, this impedes both rapid practical deployment and parameter optimization. To address these challenges, we present a learning framework that enables effective microrobot navigation policies to be trained within minutes. In the proposed framework, we develop a fully vectorized simulator with more than 10,000 artificial vascular environments, parallelizing dynamics, LiDAR-inspired perception, and feasibility checks across thousands of environments to achieve roughly 190,000 transitions per second. To achieve effectiveness in the fast training, we propose a task-shaping-regularization (TSR) reward framework. The TSR framework accelerates convergence, improves final performance, reduces action variation by at least 33.7%, and increases obstacle clearance by at least 2.1% across all evaluated scenarios. Results show that the proposed learning framework reduces training time to under 10 minutes, while supporting zero-shot deployment across distinct microrobot types and navigation scenarios. Collectively, this framework can substantially shorten the design loop and accelerate the deployment of autonomous microrobots.

</details>


### [92] [Hybrid Impedance-Admittance Control with Multi-Link Aerial Robot for Contact-Rich Surface Sliding Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01800)
*Zicheng Luo,Maolin Lei,Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Zicen Xiong,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多连杆空中机器人的混合阻抗-导纳控制策略，通过关节驱动实现导纳行为增强自适应交互，通过转子推力调节实现阻抗行为以控制滑动运动，实现未知表面上稳健且柔顺的滑动操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多连杆空中机器人在接触丰富的空中操控任务（如表面滑动）中面临挑战，需要同时具备扰动鲁棒性和对不确定表面几何的柔顺性。传统阻抗控制与导纳控制分别提供扰动抵抗和柔顺适应，但由于其相反的力-运动因果关系，无法通过同一执行源（如转子推力）同时实现。

Method: 提出混合阻抗-导纳控制策略，利用多连杆机器人的关节形态学实现功能分离：通过关节角度调节产生导纳行为以增强自适应交互，通过调节转子推力实现阻抗行为以规范滑动运动。两种控制范式在关节与转子两个不同执行源上进行结构性协调。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的混合控制策略使多连杆空中机器人在未知表面上实现了稳健且柔顺的滑动性能，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 多连杆空中机器人通过关节与转子的功能分离，能够同时发挥阻抗控制和导纳控制的互补优势，实现抵抗扰动与柔顺适应的统一，为接触丰富的空中操控任务提供了新的控制框架。

Abstract: Multi-link aerial robots can actively deform their articulated structures during flight, giving them strong potential for aerial manipulation. However, they still face substantial challenges in contact-rich aerial manipulation tasks such as surface sliding, which requires both disturbance robustness and compliance to uncertain surface geometry. Force-control strategies such as impedance and admittance control are commonly employed to address these requirements. Although impedance control can provide disturbance-resistant interaction and admittance control can offer compliant adaptation, their opposite force--motion causalities prevent their simultaneous implementation when applied through the same actuation source, such as the rotor thrusts used by conventional aerial robots. To overcome this limitation, we propose a hybrid impedance--admittance control strategy for a multi-link aerial robot. The articulated morphology enables a functional separation of force and motion regulation across joint and rotor actuation sources. In this framework, admittance behavior is generated through joint angle regulation to enhance adaptive interaction, while impedance behavior is achieved by modulating rotor thrust to regulate the sliding motion. This structural coordination allows the robot to leverage the complementary strengths of both control paradigms. As a result, the multi-link aerial robot achieves resilient and adaptive surface sliding. Experimental results demonstrate robust and compliant sliding performance on unknown surfaces.

</details>


### [93] [Bicycle Acrobatics with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00880)
*Shamel Fahmi,Arianna Ilvonen,Samuel Zapolsky,Yu-Ming Chen,Ravi Boggavarapu,Paul Drews,Ashwin Khadke,Dean Molinaro,Kaiyu Zheng,Alfred Rizzi,Gabriel Nelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用强化学习（RL）使自行车机器人学习并编排多种动态杂技动作（如跳跃、前空翻、抬前轮等），通过策略编排器协调不同技能，在仿真和真实硬件（UMV）上验证了超过连续15次自主跳跃、20次连续技能编排等成果，展示了轮式平台也能达到类腿足机器人的敏捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自行车机器人速度快且能效高，但其欠驱动、非完整约束的简化机械结构使得高难度敏捷动作难以实现。现有方法难以让自行车机器人完成高度灵活的杂技级操控，因此作者希望探索RL是否能为这类平台赋予类似腿足机器人的敏捷能力。

Method: 采用多种RL公式（如航点跟踪、位姿到达、扭转跟踪、引导跟踪和运动模仿）使机器人分别习得各自技能；引入一个策略编排器（orchestrator），基于状态依赖的触发条件在不同策略间进行切换，从而协调多种技能，实现鲁棒的长时域杂技动作序列。

Result: 在自定义自行车机器人UMV上仿真与硬件验证：可反复跨越高达1m的桌子；沿航点连续进行超过15次自主跳跃；处理未见过的多桌配置；在超过20次连续试验中执行抬前轮、跳跃、空翻、下落等连续技能编排；执行超过10次连续自主且可转向的抬轮、侧向跳跃和单轮跳落技能编排。

Conclusion: 研究表明RL能够赋予自行车机器人此前主要与腿足平台相关的敏捷性，同时保留轮式运动的速度与效率，为自行车杂技奠定了基础。

Abstract: Bicycle robots are fast and energy efficient, but their simple mechanical design and their underactuated and non-holonomic dynamics make highly agile maneuvers difficult to achieve. Here, we use Reinforcement Learning (RL) to enable a bicycle robot to learn and compose a diverse repertoire of dynamic acrobatic stunts. Using different RL formulations such as waypoint following, pose reaching, twist tracking, guided tracking, and motion imitation, the robot acquires autonomous single and multi-table forward and lateral jumps, steerable jumps, front flips, kip-ups, kip-downs, driving, wheelies, bunny hops, and three-point turns. To coordinate these behaviors, we introduce an orchestrator that transitions between policies using state-dependent triggers, enabling robust long-horizon acrobatic stunts. We validate the approach on the Ultra Mobility Vehicle (UMV), a custom bicycle robot, in simulation and hardware. The robot repeatedly traverses tables up to 1 m high, performs more than 15 consecutive autonomous jumps while following waypoints, handles previously unseen multi-table configurations, executes continuous repertoires of kipups, jumps, flips, kip-downs, over more than 20 consecutive trials, and performs more than 10 consecutive autonomous and steerable repertoires of wheelies, lateral jumps, and single-wheel jump downs. These results demonstrate that RL can endow bicycle robots with levels of agility previously associated primarily with legged platforms while preserving the speed and efficiency of wheeled locomotion, establishing a foundation for bicycle acrobatics.

</details>


### [94] [RADAR Perception for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance onboard small-scale Quadrotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01855)
*Dnyandeep Mandaokar,Bernhard Rinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于毫米波雷达的无人机快速动态避障感知-控制系统，融合交互多模型轻量级跟踪器与基于控制障碍函数的控制器，实现了在烟、雾、光照变化等复杂条件下的实时避障，端到端延迟约14ms，位置误差小于0.15m/0.93m/0.87m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机高速动态避障需要低延迟控制和可靠感知，但现有方法在感知距离、环境鲁棒性（如烟、光照变化）和实时性方面存在瓶颈，亟需一种兼具足够感知范围和低延迟的解决方案。

Method: 系统采用毫米波雷达作为感知传感器，推导并分析了关联感知范围、相对速度和控制延迟的延迟与空间边界条件，给出成功避障的充分条件；使用基于交互多模型（IMM）的轻量级目标跟踪器，并结合基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的控制器直接输出规避加速度。

Result: 在300次实验中（3种不同目标尺寸，光照明暗变化），x/y/z方向位置误差分别小于0.15m、0.93m和0.87m；在90次烟雾实验中误差分布相似。在树莓派4B上实现端到端感知到指令延迟约14ms，验证了实时可行性。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达结合交互多模型跟踪和控制障碍函数控制，能够为无人机提供鲁棒且低延迟的动态避障能力，在恶劣视觉条件（如烟雾）下仍保持良好性能，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Fast dynamic obstacle avoidance (DOA) on uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) demands not only low-latency control and actuation but also reliable perception with sufficient sensing range for accurate obstacle detection and speed estimation. This letter presents, to the best of our knowledge, the first mmWave RADAR-based perception-and-control system for fast onboard DOA. We derive and analyze latency and spatial bounds that relate sensing range, relative speed, and control delay, yielding sufficient conditions for successful avoidance. Our system adopts a lightweight tracker based on interacting multiple models and a controller based on control-barrier functions that directly outputs evasive accelerations. It achieves position errors of less than 0.15 m, 0.93 m, and 0.87 m in x, y, and z directions for 300 experiments with three different object sizes and varying visibility (light and dark), and a similar spread for 90 experiments in smoke. An onboard implementation on a Raspberry Pi 4B demonstrates real-time feasibility with an end-to-end sensing-to-command latency of approximately 14 ms. Code and the full dataset of 390 throws are available (https://tinyurl.com/radardoagit).

</details>


### [95] [Stipple: Real-Time Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Visual-Inertial Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00931)
*Kilian Northoff,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将视觉惯性跟踪系统（基于Basalt）与增量式3D Gaussian Splatting（基于Brush）相结合的实时同时跟踪与重建方法，通过利用跟踪系统产生的信息替代3DGS繁重的预处理和训练步骤，实现实时3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽然能高效渲染逼真场景，但其繁重的预处理和训练步骤使其不适合需要实时重建的机器人或XR应用。而VIO和VI-SLAM系统正好针对实时应用设计，两者具有互补性，集成它们可以实现即时反馈和场景交互。

Method: 提出一个基于Basalt的高效视觉惯性跟踪系统与基于Brush（一个用Rust编写的、GPU厂商无关的高效3DGS实现）的增量式重建方法相结合的系统。将3DGS繁重的预处理和训练步骤替换为更高效的增量训练策略，该策略直接利用视觉惯性跟踪系统生成的信息。同时提出了多项实际改进措施以提高训练流水线效率，使其能与跟踪线程并行运行并达到实时性能。

Result: 实验表明，利用视觉惯性跟踪系统的信息可以有效替代3DGS的大量预处理和训练步骤，通过增量训练策略和多项效率改进，系统能够实现实时跟踪与重建的并行运行。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了SLAM与3DGS互补性的价值，证明了利用视觉惯性跟踪系统的信息可以显著提升3DGS的训练效率，为实时3D重建提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides efficient rendering of photo-realistic scenes, but its heavy preprocessing and training steps make it a poor fit for applications that require real-time reconstruction in robotics or XR. This capability is important since it allows immediate feedback and interaction with new environments. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) and simultaneous localization and mapping (VI-SLAM) systems, on the other hand, specifically target these real-time applications, which makes them a good choice for integration with 3DGS. We propose a new method that tracks and reconstructs simultaneously in real-time by leveraging an efficient visual-inertial tracking system based on Basalt together with a novel incremental method built on top of Brush, an efficient Rust-based GPU-vendor-agnostic implementation of 3D Gaussian Splatting. We show that many of the heavy preprocessing and training steps of 3DGS can be replaced with a more efficient incremental training strategy that has direct access to the information generated by the visual-inertial tracking system. Furthermore, we propose and combine multiple practical improvements to increase the efficiency of the training pipeline and adapt it to run in real-time, parallel to the tracking thread. This work highlights the value of exploiting the complementary nature of SLAM and 3DGS, and how that can lead to promising results for real-time 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [96] [Adaptive Human-Robot Collaborative Painting Combining Preference-Based Optimization and Dynamic Motion Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01981)
*C. Cella,M. Ristic,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以人为本的协作框架，融合基于偏好的优化（PBO）和动态运动基元（DMPs），用于优化机器人辅助任务（如绘画）。系统允许操作员执行任务，机器人实时调整工件方向以匹配操作员手的朝向，通过GLISp算法迭代优化执行时间、机器人响应性等控制参数，并修改DMPs增强机器人反应行为。在异构参与者群体中验证，该策略有效减少了操作员负担并优化了过程结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助任务中，如何让机器人实时适应用户偏好和操作习惯，减少操作员体力负担，同时优化任务执行效果，是当前人机协作领域的重要挑战。

Method: 构建人机协作框架，集成两类核心技术：(1) 基于偏好的优化框架，采用GLISp算法通过人类反馈迭代优化控制参数（执行时间、机器人响应性、旋转放大系数）；(2) 修改后的动态运动基元（DMPs）以增强机器人反应行为及其对工效学要求的适应性。系统中机器人实时调整工件方向以匹配操作员手的朝向。

Result: 通过异质性参与者群体执行绘画任务进行验证，结果显示该方法有效降低了操作员负担，同时优化了过程结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明将基于偏好的优化与动态运动基元结合，能够实现机器人对操作员实时偏好和工效学需求的适应，从而提高人机协作任务中的操作舒适度和任务完成质量。

Abstract: This work presents a human-centered collaborative framework that integrates Preference-Based Optimization (PBO) and Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) to optimize robot-assisted tasks such as painting. The system allows the operator to perform the process while the robot adapts its behavior in real-time, dynamically adjusting the orientation of the piece in order to match the orientation of the operator's hand. The PBO framework leverages the GLISp algorithm to iteratively refine control parameters such as execution time, robot responsiveness, and rotation amplification through human feedback. Moreover, DMPs have been modified to enhance the reactive behavior of the robot and its adaptability to ergonomic requirements. The method was validated with a heterogeneous group of participants executing \rev{painting tasks}. The results show that our strategy effectively reduces operator effort while optimizing process outcomes.

</details>


### [97] [VertiAKD: Adaptive Off-Road Kinodynamics on Vertically Challenging Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00945)
*Tong Xu,Chenhui Pan,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VertiAKD是一个统一的框架，用于在不同车辆平台上同时进行非结构化地形条件下的运动学动力学知识迁移与适应。它学习共享的移动性表示，通过函数编码器初始化地形感知的动动力学模型，并利用在线流式观察进行无梯度重训练的周期精化，在仅需一分钟新轨迹数据的情况下，长时程预测误差较直接迁移降低34.52%，较基线降低94.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨车辆适应方法通常假设平坦地形，而地形感知的动动力学模型往往需要针对特定平台的数据采集和重新训练，无法同时处理车辆异构性与地形复杂变化，缺乏一个统一的跨车辆知识迁移框架。

Method: VertiAKD学习一种共享的移动性表示，联合编码车辆配置、轨迹转移以及局部高程和语义地形特征。面对新颖车辆和未见地形时，通过函数编码器识别最相关的移动性描述符并迁移其知识以初始化地形感知的动动力学模型，随后利用在线流式观测进行周期性精化，无需梯度重训练。评估基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench模拟器和五种物理配置的Verti-4-Wheeler平台。

Result: 仅需一分钟新轨迹数据及关联地形特征，VertiAKD在不同未见车辆配置下将长时程预测误差较直接移动性描述符迁移降低34.52%，较竞争基线降低94.43%；在仿真和物理实验中均展示了鲁棒的闭环轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: VertiAKD有效证明了三地感知的跨车辆知识迁移对于准确建模和可靠非结构化导航的有效性，能够在数据稀缺条件下实现异构车辆在复杂地形上的快速适应。

Abstract: Off-road mobility requires autonomous mobile robots to generalize across heterogeneous vehicle fleets and continuously changing terrain conditions. Existing cross-vehicle adaptation approaches generally assume flat terrain, while terrain-aware kinodynamic models often require platform-specific data collection and retraining. To this end, we propose VertiAKD, a unified framework for transferring and adapting off-road kinodynamic knowledge across diverse vehicles on geometrically and semantically complex terrain simultaneously. VertiAKD learns a shared mobility representation that jointly encodes vehicle configurations, trajectory transitions, and local elevation and semantic terrain features. Given limited data from a novel vehicle operating on unseen terrain, VertiAKD identifies the most relevant mobility descriptors and transfers their knowledge to initialize a terrain-aware kinodynamic model via function encoders, which is then periodically refined online from streaming observations without gradient-based retraining. We evaluate VertiAKD in the Verti-Bench simulator, built on the Chrono multi-physics engine, and on five physical configurations of the Verti-4-Wheeler platform. With only one minute of new trajectory data and associated terrain features, VertiAKD reduces long-horizon prediction error by up to 34.52% over direct mobility descriptor transfer across diverse unseen vehicle configurations and 94.43% over competing baselines.
